- **speckit-spec-splitter**: Avalia a descrição de uma feature (ou um spec.md já gerado) antes do /speckit.specify e decide se ela deve ser quebrada em duas ou mais specs menores, para que cada ciclo de revisão seja pequeno o bastante para ser feito de verdade. Use quando o usuário for criar uma nova spec no speckit, colar uma descrição de feature, pedir para "avaliar o tamanho da spec", "quebrar a spec" ou "dividir a feature". ([octaroxas/ai-integration](https://github.com/octaroxas/ai-integration/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/speckit-spec-splitter))

Usa questa skill ogni volta che l'utente vuole sollevare un diff a requisiti, capire o documentare COSA FA DAVVERO il codice dopo una modifica, riconciliare codice e requisiti, generare EARS da un commit o una PR, o validare un changeset — anche se non nomina esplicitamente 'SpecLift' o 'EARS'. - **specmaker**: Entry point of Ayuna specmaker, a spec-driven development workflow with phases ideate (HLD), propose (LLD, specs, ADRs), accredit (fresh-context review plus human approval), scrum (epics, features, stories, tasks), sprint (build, verify spec against code, sign-off) and retro (merge specs, archive). Use when the user mentions specmaker, wants to start, resume or triage a spec-driven change, asks "where are we" on a change, or wants to adopt specs for an existing codebase. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker))
- **specmaker-accredit**: Specmaker accredit phase. Reviews a proposed design with fresh-context "goldfish" reviewers (zoom-out, comprehension, critic, cross-artifact consistency, implementation readiness, security, codebase), triages findings to convergence with the user, records a named human's verdict and locks the approved design. Use when a specmaker change is in the accredit phase, or the user asks to review, approve or accredit a design. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-accredit))
- **specmaker-ideate**: Specmaker ideate phase. Free-flowing, challenging conversation that turns a raw idea into a High-Level Design (hld.md): problem, users, goals and non-goals, success measures, solution shape, alternatives, risks. Use when the user wants to brainstorm, explore or shape an idea inside a specmaker workspace, or a change is in the ideate phase. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-ideate))
- **specmaker-monorepo**: Optional companion to Ayuna specmaker for Nx-based polyglot monorepos built with ayunaoss/ayuna-nxtools (@ayunaio/scaffold): scaffold Go, Python and TypeScript libraries and applications, shared types (bufgen or typegen), workspace sync, per-language checks, Containerfiles and licence files. Use when the user wants to set up, extend or maintain such a monorepo, or asks how to scaffold, check or containerise a project inside one. Not needed for other repositories. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-monorepo))
- **specmaker-propose**: Specmaker propose phase. Turns an HLD (or a clear request, on the lite track) into a Low-Level Design (lld.md), behaviour specs with requirement and scenario IDs (specs/), Architecture Decision Records for framework, data and deployment choices (adrs/), and interface contracts (contracts/), confirming each section with the user. Use when a specmaker change is in the propose phase, or the user asks to design, specify or write the LLD for one. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-propose))
- **specmaker-retro**: Specmaker retro phase and drift check. Closes a change: runs a retrospective with metrics, checks living specs against code for drift, rewrites delta specs into present-tense wording, merges them into the living specs with a deterministic, self-verifying merge, promotes ADRs to the project decision log, feeds lessons back into project.md, and archives. Also use any time the user asks to check spec/code drift or spec coverage in a specmaker workspace. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-retro))
- **specmaker-scrum**: Specmaker scrum phase. Breaks an accredited design into an agile backlog (epics, features, user stories with acceptance criteria tied to spec scenario IDs, and tasks with exact file boundaries), estimates it, and plans sprints that follow the design's implementation phases. Exports to issue trackers. Use when a specmaker change is in the scrum phase, or the user asks to plan, break down or estimate the work for one. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-scrum))
- **specmaker-sprint**: Specmaker sprint phase. Runs one planned sprint: implements its stories test first inside the accredited design's file boundary, handles deviations, runs the project's quality gates, verifies spec against code with a fresh-context reviewer and a scenario-to-test trace, and asks a named human to sign off. Use when a specmaker change is in the sprint phase, or the user asks to build, implement, continue or sign off a sprint. ([ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker](https://github.com/ayunaoss/ayuna-specmaker/tree/HEAD/skills/specmaker-sprint))
